  39: const SUPPORTED_FOREIGN_RESOURCE_LOCALES = [
  40:   "en",
  41:   "es",
  42:   "fr",
  43:   "ko",
  44:   "ru",
  45: ] as const;
  46: 
  47: type SupportedForeignResourceLocale =
  48:   (typeof SUPPORTED_FOREIGN_RESOURCE_LOCALES)[number];
  49: 
  50: /* =========================================================
  51:    generateStaticParams
  52: 
  53:    璇存槑锛?
  54:    1. 濡傛灉 next.config.ts 浣跨敤 output: "export"锛岃繖閲屽繀椤诲啓
  55:    2. 杩欓噷涓€娆℃€х敓鎴愭墍鏈夊璇祫婧愪腑蹇冨叆鍙ｉ〉闈?
  56: ========================================================= */
  57: 
  58: export function generateStaticParams() {
  59:   return SUPPORTED_FOREIGN_RESOURCE_LOCALES.map((locale) => ({
  60:     locale,
  61:   }));
  62: }
  63: 
  64: /* =========================================================
  65:    椤甸潰鍙傛暟绫诲瀷
  66: 
  67:    璇存槑锛?
  68:    褰撳墠椤圭洰浣跨敤 Next.js 鏂扮増鏈啓娉曪紝params 鎸?Promise 澶勭悊銆?
  69: ========================================================= */
  70: 
  71: type LocalizedResourcesPageProps = {
  72:   params: Promise<{
  73:     locale: string;
  74:   }>;
  75: };
  76: 
  77: /* =========================================================
  78:    鍒ゆ柇褰撳墠璇█鏄惁鏄璇祫婧愪腑蹇冩敮鎸佺殑璇█
  79: ========================================================= */
  80: 
  81: function isSupportedForeignResourceLocale(
  82:   locale: string,
  83: ): locale is SupportedForeignResourceLocale {
  84:   return SUPPORTED_FOREIGN_RESOURCE_LOCALES.includes(
  85:     locale as SupportedForeignResourceLocale,
  86:   );
  87: }
  88: 
  89: /* =========================================================
  90:    generateMetadata
  91:    鐢熸垚澶栬璧勬簮涓績棣栭〉 SEO
  92: 
  93:    璇存槑锛?
  94:    1. 褰撳墠闃舵鍏堝鐢ㄨ鏍间功椤甸潰鏁版嵁
  95:    2. title 鍗曠嫭鏀规垚 Resources锛岄伩鍏嶅拰 Datasheets 椤甸潰瀹屽叏閲嶅
  96: ========================================================= */
  97: 
  98: export async function generateMetadata({
  99:   params,
 100: }: LocalizedResourcesPageProps): Promise<Metadata> {
 101:   const { locale } = await params;
 102: 
 103:   if (!isSupportedForeignResourceLocale(locale)) {
 104:     return {};
 105:   }
 106: 
 107:   const pageData = await getDatasheetsPageData(locale as DatasheetLocale);
 108: 
 109:   return {
 110:     title:
 111:       locale === "en"
 112:         ? "Resources | FOREACH"
 113:         : pageData.pageText.seo.title,
 114:     description: pageData.pageText.seo.description,
 115:   };
 116: }
 117: 
 118: /* =========================================================
 119:    LocalizedResourcesPage
 120:    澶栬璧勬簮涓績涓存椂鍏ュ彛椤?
 121: 
 122:    璇存槑锛?
 123:    1. 褰撳墠闃舵鏆傛椂澶嶇敤瑙勬牸涔︿笅杞介〉
 124:    2. 淇濊瘉 /en/resources銆?es/resources銆?fr/resources銆?ko/resources銆?ru/resources 閮借兘璁块棶
 125:    3. 鍚庢湡鐪熸鍋氳祫婧愪腑蹇冩€昏椤垫椂锛屽彧闇€瑕佹浛鎹?return 閲岀殑缁勪欢
 126: ========================================================= */
 127: 
 128: export default async function LocalizedResourcesPage({
 129:   params,
 130: }: LocalizedResourcesPageProps) {
 131:   const { locale } = await params;
 132: 
 133:   if (!isSupportedForeignResourceLocale(locale)) {
 134:     notFound();
 135:   }
 136: 
 137:   const pageData = await getDatasheetsPageData(locale as DatasheetLocale);
 138: 
 139:   return (
 140:     <DatasheetsClient
 141:       pageText={pageData.pageText}
 142:       filterOptions={pageData.filterOptions}
 143:       datasheetItems={pageData.datasheetItems}
 144:     />
 145:   );
 146: }
